function Initu(x, eta, mu)
  output_size(ui) = 5;
  ui[0] = mu[0]*mu[3]*mu[3]*((0.0625*cos(2.0*x[0]) + 0.0625*cos(2.0*x[1]))*(cos(2.0*x[2]) + 2.0) + 1.0/(mu[0]*mu[3]*mu[3]));
  ui[1] = mu[0]*mu[3]*mu[3]*((0.0625*cos(2.0*x[0]) + 0.0625*cos(2.0*x[1]))*(cos(2.0*x[2]) + 2.0) + 1.0/(mu[0]*mu[3]*mu[3]))*sin(1.0*x[0])*cos(1.0*x[1])*cos(1.0*x[2]);
  ui[2] = -mu[0]*mu[3]*mu[3]*((0.0625*cos(2.0*x[0]) + 0.0625*cos(2.0*x[1]))*(cos(2.0*x[2]) + 2.0) + 1.0/(mu[0]*mu[3]*mu[3]))*sin(1.0*x[1])*cos(1.0*x[0])*cos(1.0*x[2]);
  ui[3] = 0;
  ui[4] = 0.5*mu[0]*mu[3]*mu[3]*((0.0625*cos(2.0*x[0]) + 0.0625*cos(2.0*x[1]))*(cos(2.0*x[2]) + 2.0) + 1.0/(mu[0]*mu[3]*mu[3]))*(sin(1.0*x[0])*sin(1.0*x[0])*cos(1.0*x[1])*cos(1.0*x[1])*cos(1.0*x[2])*cos(1.0*x[2]) + sin(1.0*x[1])*sin(1.0*x[1])*cos(1.0*x[0])*cos(1.0*x[0])*cos(1.0*x[2])*cos(1.0*x[2])) + ((0.0625*cos(2.0*x[0]) + 0.0625*cos(2.0*x[1]))*(cos(2.0*x[2]) + 2.0) + 1.0/(mu[0]*mu[3]*mu[3]))/(mu[0] - 1.0);
end

function Initv(x, eta, mu)
  output_size(vi) = 1;
  vi[0] = 0.0;
end
